Qualified experimental child–PM relationships

Type 2 resistant starch (tested aged-mouse exposure) → BRS5-FM1-PM2

Aged female mice, sixteen-week high-fat diet with the tested high-amylose RS2 material: lower serum/faecal LPS and increased colonic mucin.

No human efficacy or ordinary-food equivalence; preserve null colon histology/length and cytokine findings. The 20% dietary material is not 20% pure RS2.

Apple pectin (studied preparation) → BRS5-FM3-PM8

Studied apple-pectin exposures in cultures and mice: altered microbial tryptophan-metabolite partitioning.

Preserve culture reagent versus mouse diet distinctions, below-detection ILA/IPA in FMT, nonsignificant faecal tryptophan change and no serum tryptophan difference. No whole-apple equivalence or human neurotransmitter benefit.
